HealthDetector.cs 

using System;

namespace Lab6
{
    public delegate void HealthEmergencyHandler(string alertMessage, string username);

    class HealthDetector
    {
        private Random _seed;
        public event HealthEmergencyHandler OnHealthEmergency = null!;

        public HealthDetector()
        {
            Seed = new Random();
        }

        public Random Seed
        {
            get => _seed;
            set
            {
                if (value == null)
                    throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(Seed), "Random seed cannot be null.");
                _seed = value;
            }
        }

        public void AnalyzeUserState(User user, bool simulateEmergency = false)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"[Health Detector] Scanning face and vitals for {user.Username}...");

            double temp = ScanTemp;
            int pulse = ScanPulse;
            int sysPressure = ScanSysPressure;
            int diaPressure = ScanDiaPressure;
            bool facialAsymmetry = ScanFacialAsymmetry();
            string mood = ScanMood();

            // Моделювання критичних ситуацій
            if (simulateEmergency)
            {
                int emergencyType = Seed.Next(1, 5); // Випадково обираємо 1 з 4 сценаріїв

                switch (emergencyType)
                {
                    case 1: // Серцевий напад / Тахікардія
                        pulse = Seed.Next(150, 190);
                        sysPressure = Seed.Next(180, 220);
                        break;
                    case 2: // Критична температура (Лихоманка)
                        temp = Math.Round(39.6 + (Seed.NextDouble() * 1.5), 1);
                        pulse = Seed.Next(100, 130);
                        break;
                    case 3: // Ознаки інсульту
                        sysPressure = Seed.Next(170, 200);
                        facialAsymmetry = true;
                        break;
                    case 4: // Втрата свідомості / Гіпотонія
                        pulse = Seed.Next(35, 50);
                        sysPressure = Seed.Next(70, 85);
                        diaPressure = Seed.Next(40, 50);
                        break;
                }
            }

            Console.WriteLine($" - Body Temp: {temp}°C");
            Console.WriteLine($" - Pulse: {pulse} bpm");
            Console.WriteLine($" - Blood Pressure: {sysPressure}/{diaPressure} mmHg");
            Console.WriteLine($" - Emotion/Mood: {mood}");
            if (facialAsymmetry)
                Console.WriteLine($" - Facial Symmetry: [WARNING] Asymmetry Detected!");

            // 1. Перевірка на критичні для життя стани
            string alertMessage = "";

            if (facialAsymmetry)
                alertMessage = "STROKE SYMPTOMS DETECTED (Facial Asymmetry)!";
            else if (temp > 39.5)
                alertMessage = "CRITICAL HYPERTHERMIA (Fever > 39.5°C)!";
            else if (pulse > 140 || sysPressure > 180)
                alertMessage = "HEART ATTACK RISK / HYPERTENSIVE CRISIS!";
            else if (pulse < 45 || sysPressure < 85)
                alertMessage = "SEVERE HYPOTENSION (Risk of Fainting)!";

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(alertMessage))
            {
                OnHealthEmergency?.Invoke(alertMessage, user.Username);
                throw new CriticalHealthException($"Health critical failure for: {user.Username}. Cause: {alertMessage}");
            }

            // 2. Якщо стан не критичний, даємо розумні поради на основі настрою та легких відхилень
            Console.WriteLine("[Health Detector] Vitals are within acceptable limits.");

            if (temp > 37.2 && temp <= 39.5)
            {
                Console.WriteLine("[Smart Suggestion] You have a slight fever. Drink warm fluids and rest.");
            }

            switch (mood)
            {
                case "Tired":
                    Console.WriteLine("[Smart Suggestion] You look exhausted. Dimming mirror lights. Recommend sleeping early.");
                    break;
                case "Stressed":
                    Console.WriteLine("[Smart Suggestion] Elevated stress detected. Playing relaxing ambient music...");
                    break;
                case "Sad":
                    Console.WriteLine("[Smart Suggestion] Showing your favorite motivational quotes on the mirror display.");
                    break;
                case "Happy":
                    Console.WriteLine("[Smart Suggestion] You look great today! Keep smiling.");
                    break;
            }
        }

        private double ScanTemp => Math.Round(36.2 + (Seed.NextDouble() * 1.0), 1);
        private int ScanPulse => Seed.Next(60, 100);
        private int ScanSysPressure => Seed.Next(110, 130);
        private int ScanDiaPressure => Seed.Next(70, 85);
        private bool ScanFacialAsymmetry()
        {
            Seed.Next(0, 100);
            if (Seed.Next(0, 100) < 1)
                return true;
            return false;
        }
        private string ScanMood()
        {
            int moodValue = Seed.Next(1, 6);
            return moodValue switch
            {
                1 => "Happy",
                2 => "Sad",
                3 => "Tired",
                4 => "Stressed",
                _ => "Neutral"
            };
        }
    }
}
Network.cs 

using System;

namespace Lab6
{
    class NetworkModule
    {
        private bool _turnedOn;
        private bool _isWifiConnected;
        private bool _isBluetoothPaired;

        public NetworkModule()
        {
            TurnedOn = false;
            IsWifiConnected = false;
            IsBluetoothPaired = false;
        }

        public bool TurnedOn { get => _turnedOn; private set => _turnedOn = value; }
        public bool IsWifiConnected { get => _isWifiConnected; set => _isWifiConnected = value; }
        public bool IsBluetoothPaired { get => _isBluetoothPaired; set => _isBluetoothPaired = value; }

        // --- УПРАВЛІННЯ ЖИВЛЕННЯМ МОДУЛЯ ---

        public void TurnOn()
        {
            if (!TurnedOn)
            {
                TurnedOn = true;
                Console.WriteLine("[Network] Network module turned ON.");
            }
            else
            {
                Console.WriteLine("[Network] Network module is already ON.");
            }
        }

        public void TurnOff()
        {
            if (TurnedOn)
            {
                TurnedOn = false;
                IsWifiConnected = false;
                IsBluetoothPaired = false;
                Console.WriteLine("[Network] Network module turned OFF.");
            }
            else
            {
                Console.WriteLine("[Network] Network module is already OFF.");
            }
        }

        // --- РОБОТА З WI-FI ---

        public void ConnectWiFi(string ssid = null!)
        {
            try
            {
                if (!TurnedOn)
                {
                    throw new NetworkUnavailableException("Cannot connect to Wi-Fi. Module is OFF.");
                }

                if (ssid == null)
                {
                    Console.Write("[Network] Enter Wi-Fi network name (press Enter for 'Home_Smart_Network'): ");
                    ssid = Console.ReadLine() ?? "";
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ssid))
                        ssid = "Home_Smart_Network";
                }

                IsWifiConnected = true;
                Console.WriteLine($"[Network] Connected to Wi-Fi: {ssid}");
            }
            catch (NetworkUnavailableException exception)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"[Network] Error: {exception.Message}");
            }
        }

        public void DisconnectWiFi()
        {
            if (IsWifiConnected)
            {
                IsWifiConnected = false;
                Console.WriteLine("[Network] Disconnected from Wi-Fi.");
            }
            else
            {
                Console.WriteLine("[Network] Wi-Fi is already disconnected.");
            }
        }

        // --- РОБОТА З BLUETOOTH ---

        public void PairBluetooth(string deviceName = null!)
        {
            try
            {
                if (!TurnedOn)
                {
                    throw new NetworkUnavailableException("Cannot pair Bluetooth. Module is OFF.");
                }

                if (deviceName == null)
                {
                    Console.Write("[Network] Enter Bluetooth device name (press Enter for 'Smartphone'): ");
                    deviceName = Console.ReadLine() ?? "";
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(deviceName))
                        deviceName = "Smartphone";
                }

                IsBluetoothPaired = true;
                Console.WriteLine($"[Network] Bluetooth paired with {deviceName}");
            }
            catch (NetworkUnavailableException exception)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"[Network] Error: {exception.Message}");
            }
        }

        public void UnpairBluetooth()
        {
            if (IsBluetoothPaired)
            {
                IsBluetoothPaired = false;
                Console.WriteLine("[Network] Bluetooth unpaired.");
            }
            else
            {
                Console.WriteLine("[Network] Bluetooth is not paired with any device.");
            }
        }

        // --- ПЕРЕДАЧА ДАНИХ ---

        public void TransmitData(string data = null!)
        {
            try
            {
                if (!TurnedOn)
                {
                    throw new NetworkUnavailableException("Cannot transmit data. Module is OFF.");
                }

                if (data == null)
                {
                    Console.Write("[Network] Enter text data to transmit: ");
                    data = Console.ReadLine() ?? "";
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(data))
                        data = "some important data...";
                }

                if (IsWifiConnected || IsBluetoothPaired)
                    Console.WriteLine($"[Network] Transmitting: {data}");
                else
                    throw new NetworkUnavailableException("No active connection to transmit data.");
            }
            catch (NetworkUnavailableException exception)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"[Network] Error: {exception.Message}");
            }
        }

        public void ReceivePhoto(string friendName = null!)
        {
            try
            {
                if (!TurnedOn)
                {
                    throw new NetworkUnavailableException("Cannot receive data. Module is OFF.");
                }

                if (friendName == null)
                {
                    Console.Write("[Network] Enter friend's name: ");
                    friendName = Console.ReadLine() ?? "";
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(friendName))
                        friendName = "Best Friend";
                }

                if (IsWifiConnected)
                {
                    Console.WriteLine($"[Network] Received a new photo from {friendName}. Displaying on mirror...\n");
                    DrawBaseFace();
                }
                else
                    throw new NetworkUnavailableException("No Wi-Fi connection to receive photo.");
            }
            catch (NetworkUnavailableException exception)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"[Network] Error: {exception.Message}");
            }
        }

        private void DrawBaseFace()
        {
            Console.WriteLine("       .-------.       ");
            Console.WriteLine("     /           \\     ");
            Console.WriteLine("    |    ^   ^    |    ");
            Console.WriteLine("    |      v      |    ");
            Console.WriteLine("     \\   \\___/   /     ");
            Console.WriteLine("       `-------`       ");
        }
    }
}
StylingSim.cs 

using System;

namespace Lab6
{
    class StylingSimulator
    {
        public void SimulateHairstyle(Camera camera, User user)
        {
            try
            {
                if (!camera.IsActive)
                    throw new CameraNotFoundException("Camera is off. Cannot take photo.");


                bool exit = false;

                Console.WriteLine($"[Styling Simulator] Starting AR overlay for {user.Username}...");

                while (!exit)
                {
                    Console.WriteLine("[Styling Simulator] Please select a virtual style to try on:");
                    Console.WriteLine("======================================");
                    Console.WriteLine(" 1. Stylish Short Hair");
                    Console.WriteLine(" 2. Long Wavy Hair");
                    Console.WriteLine(" 3. Voluminous Curls");
                    Console.WriteLine(" 4. Punk Mohawk");
                    Console.WriteLine(" 5. Elegant Top Hat");
                    Console.WriteLine(" 6. Cool Sunglasses");
                    Console.WriteLine(" 7. Cowboy Hat");
                    Console.WriteLine(" 0. Exit styling mode");
                    Console.WriteLine("======================================");
                    Console.Write("Your choice: ");

                    string choice = Console.ReadKey().KeyChar.ToString()!;

                    if (choice == "0")
                    {
                        Console.WriteLine("\n\n[Styling Simulator] Exiting AR mode.");
                        exit = true;
                        return;
                    }

                    Console.WriteLine("\n\n[AR Display] Rendering projection...\n");

                    string currentArt = "";

                    switch (choice)
                    {
                        case "1": currentArt = GetShortHair(); break;
                        case "2": currentArt = GetLongHair(); break;
                        case "3": currentArt = GetCurls(); break;
                        case "4": currentArt = GetMohawk(); break;
                        case "5": currentArt = GetTopHat(); break;
                        case "6": currentArt = GetSunglasses(); break;
                        case "7": currentArt = GetCowboyHat(); break;
                        default: Console.WriteLine("[System Error] Unknown selection."); break;
                    }

                    Console.WriteLine(currentArt);

                    Console.Write("\n[System] Do you want to save this photo? (y/n): ");
                    string saveChoice = Console.ReadKey().KeyChar.ToString();
                    if (saveChoice.ToLower() == "y")
                        camera.TakePhoto(currentArt, user.Username);

                    Console.WriteLine("\n------------------------------------------------");
                } 
            }
            catch (CameraNotFoundException exception)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"[Camera Error] {exception.Message}");
            }
        }

        // ASCII АРТИ

        private string GetBaseFace()
        {
            return string.Join("\n", new[] {
                "       .-------.       ",
                "     /           \\     ",
                "    |    ^   ^    |    ",
                "    |      v      |    ",
                "     \\   \\___/   /     ",
                "       `-------`       "
            });
        }

        private string GetShortHair()
        {
            return string.Join("\n", new[] {
                "       /////////       ",
                "     //         \\\\     ",
                "    |    ^   ^    |    ",
                "    |      v      |    ",
                "     \\   \\___/   /     ",
                "       `-------`       "
            });
        }

        private string GetLongHair()
        {
            return string.Join("\n", new[] {
                "      ( (     ) )      ",
                "    (   .-----.   )    ",
                "   (  /         \\  )   ",
                "   | |   ^   ^   | |   ",
                "   ( |     v     | )   ",
                "    )|   \\___/   |(    ",
                "   (  \\         /  )   ",
                "        `-----`        "
            });
        }

        private string GetCurls()
        {
            return string.Join("\n", new[] {
                "      @@@@@@@@@@@      ",
                "    @@@@@@@@@@@@@@@    ",
                "   @@@  .-----.  @@@   ",
                "   @@ /         \\ @@   ",
                "   @ |   ^   ^   | @   ",
                "   @ |     v     | @   ",
                "      \\  \\___/  /      ",
                "       `-------`       "
            });
        }

        private string GetMohawk()
        {
            return string.Join("\n", new[] {
                "          |||          ",
                "         |||||         ",
                "        |||||||        ",
                "       .-------.       ",
                "     /           \\     ",
                "    |    ^   ^    |    ",
                "    |      v      |    ",
                "     \\   \\___/   /     ",
                "       `-------`       "
            });
        }

        private string GetTopHat()
        {
            return string.Join("\n", new[] {
                "          ___          ",
                "        _|___|_        ",
                "       |       |       ",
                "      ===========      ",
                "     /           \\     ",
                "    |    ^   ^    |    ",
                "    |      v      |    ",
                "     \\   \\___/   /     ",
                "       `-------`       "
            });
        }

        private string GetSunglasses()
        {
            return string.Join("\n", new[] {
                "       .-------.       ",
                "     /           \\     ",
                "    |  [===_===]  |    ",
                "    |      v      |    ",
                "     \\   \\___/   /     ",
                "       `-------`       "
            });
        }

        private string GetCowboyHat()
        {
            return string.Join("\n", new[] {
                "        _______        ",
                "       /       \\       ",
                "   ___|_________|___   ",
                "  /_________________\\  ",
                "     /           \\     ",
                "    |    ^   ^    |    ",
                "    |      v      |    ",
                "     \\   \\___/   /     ",
                "       `-------`       "
            });
        }
    }
}
Weather.cs 

using System;

namespace Lab6
{
    class WeatherModule
    {
        private Random random;

        // Масиви для збереження прогнозу на 5 днів
        private int[] weeklyTemperatures = new int[5];
        private string[] weeklyConditions = new string[5];

        public WeatherModule()
        {
            random = new Random();
        }

        private void GenerateRealisticWeeklyForecast()
        {
            // Визначаємо "сезон" для цього запуску
            int baseTemp = random.Next(-15, 33);

            for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
            {
                // Температура може коливатися на +- 4 градуси від базової кожного дня
                int temp = baseTemp + random.Next(-4, 5);
                weeklyTemperatures[i] = temp;

                if (temp <= 0)
                {
                    // Зима / Мороз
                    string[] coldConditions = { "Sunny", "Cloudy", "Snowy", "Blizzard" };
                    weeklyConditions[i] = coldConditions[random.Next(coldConditions.Length)];
                }
                else if (temp > 0 && temp <= 12)
                {
                    // Холодна осінь / весна
                    string[] chillyConditions = { "Sunny", "Cloudy", "Rainy", "Windy" };
                    weeklyConditions[i] = chillyConditions[random.Next(chillyConditions.Length)];
                }
                else if (temp > 12 && temp <= 25)
                {
                    // Тепла погода
                    string[] warmConditions = { "Sunny", "Partly Cloudy", "Light Rain", "Windy" };
                    weeklyConditions[i] = warmConditions[random.Next(warmConditions.Length)];
                }
                else
                {
                    // Спека (Можливі літні грози)
                    string[] hotConditions = { "Sunny", "Hot & Clear", "Partly Cloudy", "Thunderstorm" };
                    weeklyConditions[i] = hotConditions[random.Next(hotConditions.Length)];
                }
            }
        }

        public void ShowForecast(int dayOffset)
        {
            GenerateRealisticWeeklyForecast();

            try
            {
                int temp = weeklyTemperatures[dayOffset];
                string condition = weeklyConditions[dayOffset];

                string dayLabel = dayOffset == 0 ? "today" : $"day +{dayOffset}";
                Console.WriteLine($"[Weather] Forecast for {dayLabel}: {condition}, {temp}°C");

                // Виводимо підказки ТІЛЬКИ для сьогоднішнього дня
                if (dayOffset == 0)
                {
                    if (condition == "Rain" || condition == "Thunderstorm")
                    {
                        Console.WriteLine("[Weather] Suggestion: Don't forget to take an umbrella!");
                    }
                    else if (condition == "Snow" || condition == "Blizzard")
                    {
                        Console.WriteLine("[Weather] Suggestion: Wear warm boots, it's snowy outside!");
                    }

                    if (temp < 0)
                    {
                        Console.WriteLine("[Weather] Suggestion: Dress warmly, it's freezing!");
                    }
                    else if (temp > 25 && (condition == "Sunny" || condition == "Hot"))
                    {
                        Console.WriteLine("[Weather] Suggestion: High UV index. Put on sunscreen and wear a hat!");
                    }
                }
            }
            catch (IndexOutOfRangeException exception)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"[Weather Error] Cannot load forecast for day +{dayOffset}. {exception.Message}");
                Console.WriteLine("[Weather] Only 5-day forecast is available.");
            }
        }
    }
}
Exceptions.cs 

using System;

namespace Lab6
{
    // Виключення: Критичний стан здоров'я людини
    public class CriticalHealthException : Exception
    {
        public CriticalHealthException(string message) : base(message) { }
    }

    // Виключення: Дзеркало розрядилось
    public class LowBatteryException : Exception
    {
        public LowBatteryException(string message) : base(message) { }
    }

    // Виключення: Камера вимкнена
    public class CameraNotFoundException : Exception
    {
        public CameraNotFoundException(string message) : base(message) { }
    }

    // Виключення: Відсутність мережевого з'єднання
    public class NetworkUnavailableException : Exception
    {
        public NetworkUnavailableException(string message) : base(message) { }
    }
}

Аналіз: Похідний клас VoiceAssistant успішно розпізнає ключові слова ("weather", "check me") у введених
        користувачем реченнях. Завдяки використанню словника Dictionary, ключі зіставляються з делегатами типу Action,
        які, у свою чергу, викликають відповідні методи базового класу SmartMirror. Модуль здоров'я також
        успішно визначає втому і виводить відповідну розумну пораду.
